ChangeOperation {\n    53→\treadonly type = BufferOperationType.Change;\n    54→\tconstructor(\n    55→\t\tpublic readonly args: Proto.FileCodeEdits\n    56→\t) { }\n    57→}\n    58→\n    59→type BufferOperation = CloseOperation | OpenOperation | ChangeOperation;\n    60→\n    61→/**\n    62→ * Manages synchronization of buffers with the TS server.\n    63→ *\n    64→ * If supported, batches together file changes. This allows the TS server to more efficiently process changes.\n    65→ */\n    66→class BufferSynchronizer {\n    67→\n    68→\tprivate readonly _pending: ResourceMap<BufferOperation>;\n    69→\n    70→\tconstructor(\n    71→\t\tprivate readonly client: ITypeScriptServiceClient,\n    72→\t\tpathNormalizer: (path: vscode.Uri) => string | undefined,\n    73→\t\tonCaseInsensitiveFileSystem: 113→\t\t}\n   114→\n   115→\t\tthis.flush();\n   116→\t}\n   117→\n   118→\tprivate flush() {\n   119→\t\tif (this._pending.size > 0) {\n   120→\t\t\tconst closedFiles: string[] = [];\n   121→\t\t\tconst openFiles: Proto.OpenRequestArgs[] = [];\n   122→\t\t\tconst changedFiles: Proto.FileCodeEdits[] = [];\n   123→\t\t\tfor (const change of this._pending.values()) {\n   124→\t\t\t\tswitch (change.type) {\n   125→\t\t\t\t\tcase BufferOperationType.Change: changedFiles.push(change.args); break;\n   126→\t\t\t\t\tcase BufferOperationType.Open: 135→\tprivate updatePending(resource: vscode.Uri, op: BufferOperation): boolean {\n   136→\t\tswitch (op.type) {\n   137→\t\t\tcase BufferOperationType.Close: {\n   138→\t\t\t\tconst existing = 274→\tprivate constructor(\n   275→\t\tprivate readonly client: ITypeScriptServiceClient,\n   276→\t\tpublic readonly files: ResourceMap<void | vscode.Range[]>,\n   277→\t\tonDone: () => void\n Here's a summary of the implementation:\n\n## Summary\n\n**Problem:** After switching Git branches, stale TypeScript diagnostics remained in the Problems panel. Files that no longer had errors still showed old errors until the user manually edited them. The diagnostics pipeline only refreshed on in-editor text changes (`onDidChangeTextDocument`), missing file-on-disk changes from Git operations.\n\n**Root Cause:** In `BufferSyncSupport.listen()` (`extensions/typescript-language-features/src/tsServer/bufferSyncSupport.ts:513`), only these events were listened to:\n- `onDidOpenTextDocument` - document opened in editor\n- `onDidCloseTextDocument` - document closed\n- `onDidChangeTextDocument` - in-editor text changes\n- `onDidChangeVisibleTextEditors` - editor visibility changes\n\nNone of these fire when files change on disk (e.g., `git checkout` modifying file contents).\n\n**Fix:** Added `listenForDiskFileChanges()` method that creates a `FileSystemWatcher` for TypeScript/JavaScript files (`**/{*.ts,*.tsx,*.js,*.jsx}`). When a watched file changes, is created, or is deleted on disk:\n1. It checks if the file is currently synced (open in editor) via `this.handles(uri)`\n2. If so, it calls `this.requestAllDiagnostics()` to refresh diagnostics for all synced buffers (necessary because cross-file dependencies mean any file change can affect diagnostics in other files)\n3. For deleted files, it also fires `this._onDelete.fire(uri)` to clear diagnostics for the deleted file\n\n**Performance considerations:**\n- The watcher only triggers re-diagnostics for files that are currently synced (open in editor tabs), not for all file changes\n- `requestAllDiagnostics()` already has built-in debouncing via `triggerDiagnostics(delay)` (200ms default) and the `diagnosticDelayer` (300ms)\n- The `FileSystemWatcher` is properly registered with `this._register()` for cleanup\n\n**Test results:** All 37 diagnostic-related tests pass:\n- 9 Marker Service tests\n- 18 ExtHostDiagnostics tests\n- 3 MainThreadDiagnostics tests\n- 7 MarkersModel tests"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":756,"cache_read_input_tokens":140441,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":756},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ee76579e-c1b8-4ae0-8854-74b7085c9da3","uuid":"14723aeb-4208-4bfa-a289-4fbfbc089feb"}
